# Classies Chronicles — Updated Master Episode Framework

**Proposed document:** `Classies_Chronicles_Master_Episode_Framework_v0.03.00`  
**Status:** Proposed update to `Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt`  
**Scope:** A generic, AI-independent framework for generating **one daily chronicle at a time**; ten chronicles are later manually compiled into one Classies Chronicles episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The original proposal has a strong production concept—ten working days, rotating organisational roles, Edubeam missions, Bible study, Health & Safety, news, diaries and Koware operations—but it reads mainly as a fixed daily schedule. The main update is to convert it into a **repository-driven narrative system**: each AI must research the supplied links and archives, determine the current episode day, and generate only that day’s chronicle. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## Core production rules

1. **One episode = ten working days.** It always begins on a Monday and ends on the Friday of the following week. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
2. **One chronicle per working day.** Unless explicitly instructed, an AI must generate only the chronicle for the current day—not all ten parts. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
3. **No hard-coded dates, episode numbers, missions or destinations.** The generating AI must derive them from the shared repositories, archives, the Millennium Timezone endpoint and continuity records. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
4. **Manual compilation follows generation.** The ten approved daily chronicles are collected, edited for continuity, and combined into one episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
5. **The story belongs to the Classies Chronicles canon, not to the AI.** New material must be classified and must not silently become canon. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## Calendar and episode positioning

Each year contains one season, divided into five chapters/terms:

| Chapter/Term | Approximate period |
|---|---|
| Term 1 | Mid-January to mid-March |
| Term 2 | Mid-March to mid-May |
| Term 3 | June and July |
| Term 4 | Mid-August to mid-October |
| Term 5 | Mid-October to mid-December |

Each term has eight weeks; each episode occupies two weeks; each chronicle represents one working day. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### Determining the current day

Before writing, the AI must call:

```text
https://buzzjuice.net/shared/millennium-timezone/millennium-probe.php?probe=now
```

It must use `phase_start_iso` and `phase_end_iso` to identify the current term, then determine the **first Monday of that term**. The first episode of the term begins on that Monday. The AI must then calculate the current date’s position as:

- **Day 1–5:** first week of the current episode.
- **Day 6–10:** second week of the current episode.
- **Saturday/Sunday:** no compulsory daily chronicle; the AI must not generate a weekday chronicle unless specifically instructed. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The endpoint must also be used to establish the current day of the episode before generating any chronicle. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## Source hierarchy

The AI must consult sources in this order of authority:

| Priority | Source | Use |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Established Classies Chronicles canon and archives | Character continuity, unresolved plots, prior missions, world rules  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 2 | Koware Management repository and organisational chart | Roles, aliases, check days, projects, policies and operations  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 3 | Eternal Dreamfall reading plan | The week’s assigned reading material  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 4 | YouVersion Verse of the Day | The actual verse for the publication date; never invented  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 5 | Buzzjuice Health & Safety materials | One relevant practical safety theme per chronicle  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 6 | Millennium Timezone endpoints | Historical date, probe date, current episode positioning  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 7 | Verified current, historical and clearly labelled fictional-future news | Context, headlines and thematic parallels  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 8 | Daily diary notes | Personal texture and narrative mirroring, without treating fiction as fact  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |

If a source is inaccessible, the AI must state the limitation rather than invent content. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## Narrative spine

Every episode must have one evolving storyline derived from the archives, Koware’s current work, the reading material, selected headlines and the Edubeam assignment. The ten chronicles must advance that storyline rather than repeat the same routine with different dates. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

Recommended ten-day progression:

| Day | Narrative function |
|---|---|
| 1 — Monday | Establish setting, characters, briefing, assignment and central situation  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 2 — Tuesday | Begin exploration, research or deployment  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 3 — Wednesday | Introduce discovery, complication or deeper involvement  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 4 — Thursday | Review progress, connect findings to Koware, follow-up briefing  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 5 — Friday | Close the first week; prepare reports, weekend continuity and unresolved threads  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 6 — Monday | Resume after the weekend; weekly briefing and renewed focus  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 7 — Tuesday | Advance investigation and character development  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 8 — Wednesday | Converge storylines and expose consequences  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 9 — Thursday | Major decision, retrieval, report preparation or turning point  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 10 — Friday | Conclude or hand over the episode; integrate findings and reveal next-team continuity  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |

The conclusion may resolve the episode or deliberately leave a continuity thread, but it must not force an artificial ending. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## Daily chronicle structure

Each daily chronicle should be an immersive narrative of roughly 3–5 minutes’ reading time, written as one continuous story rather than separated devotional, safety, news and corporate sections. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

### Required elements

- **Opening atmosphere:** early morning, domestic environment, commute, workstation or Edubeam chamber as appropriate. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- **Spiritual thread:** the day’s Eternal Dreamfall reading and verified Verse of the Day, woven into reflection, dialogue or decision-making. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- **Health & Safety thread:** one practical lesson connected naturally to the story. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- **News thread:** selected historical, current and probe-period headlines, clearly distinguished from fictional future reporting. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- **Koware thread:** real projects, roles, meetings, products, services or operational needs. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- **Edubeam thread:** mission status, discoveries, communication, risks, retrieval or reporting. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- **Closing hook:** a question, riddle, decision point, unresolved observation or next-day development. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### Storytelling pipeline

The AI should develop the day’s material through this sequence:

1. **Eternal Dreamfall Bible Study** — identify and contextualise the assigned reading. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
2. **Eternal Dreamfall Chronicle** — connect the reading’s themes to the characters and situation. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
3. **Refreshed Eternal Dreamfall** — integrate one verified Health & Safety lesson. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
4. **Headlined Eternal Dreamfall** — integrate curated historical, current and probe-period headlines. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
5. **Classies Eternal Dreamfall** — integrate archives, diary notes and established characters. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
6. **Classies Chronicles** — produce the final coherent daily story within the Koware ecosystem. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## Eternal Dreamfall reading plan

The reading plan is a **weekly** plan, not a fixed verse-per-day plan. It begins with ancient Near Eastern and early Israelite materials, proceeds through canonical biblical books, then covers Second Temple, apocryphal, pseudepigraphal, Qumran, early Christian and later extra-canonical traditions across 250 weeks. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Classies_Chronicles/refs/heads/main/Eternal_Dreamfall_Bible_Study/list_of_biblical_texts-5_year_reading_plan-1.txt)

For each chronicle, the AI must:

- Determine the correct week from the plan and the episode’s position in the five-year cycle. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Select an appropriate passage or thematic focus from that week’s assigned material. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Clearly identify the selected text and explain why it was chosen. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Preserve the plan’s sequence; do not invent daily allocations. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Treat non-canonical ancient texts as historical, literary or comparative material according to their nature, not automatically as scripture. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Classies_Chronicles/refs/heads/main/Eternal_Dreamfall_Bible_Study/list_of_biblical_texts-5_year_reading_plan-1.txt)

## Role spotlight and check days

The organisational chart assigns aliases and check days to roles. These check days are **episode-day positions**, not calendar dates. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)

Examples from the chart:

- Stakeholders, Shareholders, Governing Committee and Board of Directors: Day 1. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)
- Corporate Secretary, Department Secretary and Corporate Administrator: Day 2. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)
- Administrative Auditor, Accounts & Finance Executive and Assets & Records Coordinator: Day 3. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)
- Corporate Manager and liaison personnel: Day 4. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)
- Creative, design, media and podcast roles: Days 6–7. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)
- R&D Director, R&D Lab Specialist and Liaison Technician: Day 11. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)
- Events, tours, community outreach and booking roles: Day 20. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)

### Applying check days

- A check day of **1–10** applies within the current episode.
- A check day of **11–20** applies to the equivalent day of the **next episode**: Day 11 means Day 1 of the next episode; Day 20 means Day 10 of the next episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Each chronicle should spotlight **one or two** roles, with a maximum of four only when the story genuinely requires it. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- The spotlight should reveal the role’s purpose, workflow, relationship to Edubeam teams, current challenges and potential improvements. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- A role may recur when the storyline, headlines or mission makes that appropriate; roles must not be forced into a scene merely to meet a quota. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- The Corporate Manager’s routine must remain variable and diary-driven rather than identical every day. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## Edubeam canon

The technology is canonically named **Edubeam** in the initial proposal; the updated framework retains that name. It is a controlled teleportation-and-temporal-probe system operated by Koware’s Blue Crown R&D function. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### Operational rules

- The R&D Lab Specialist fine-tunes the Edubeam and updates deployed personnel with the day’s timezone, reading, verse, safety guidance and mission details. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Deployed teams must be at the assigned coordinates and time to receive updates or be retrieved. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Missions should have a clear research, observation, safety, product-development or organisational purpose. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- The team’s charge, equipment, communications, local conditions and retrieval status must remain internally consistent. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- The Edubeam may materialise objects, but larger objects and greater temporal distance increase risk; the established 250-year safe threshold should be respected unless a later approved canon changes it. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Storms, solar activity, electromagnetic disturbance and other unstable conditions are operational hazards. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- A missed retrieval, refusal to return, arrest, disappearance or smuggling incident may become a subplot, but only where supported by continuity and handled without gratuitous content. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### Mission status

Before writing, the AI must establish whether the team is:

- Preparing for deployment.
- Newly deployed.
- Continuing an assignment.
- Experiencing a communications or equipment anomaly.
- Preparing to return.
- Returning or undergoing decontamination and assessment.
- Preparing reports or briefing the next team. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## News integration

For each chronicle, the AI should curate headlines across three time layers:

1. **Historical headlines** for the Millennium Timezone historical date and Enka’s Millennium Timezone location. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
2. **Current headlines** for the Koware headquarters location and global context. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
3. **Probe headlines** for the Edubeam probe date and probe location. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The AI should then select:

- Three related or attention-grabbing global headlines: one historical, one current, one probe-period. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Five related local headlines: one historical, three current, one probe-period. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

Historical news must be accurate to its period; future or probe-period news must be explicitly fictional or speculative unless a verified source exists. The AI must not invent quotations, reports, events or publication dates. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## Diary mirroring

Diary notes add lived texture and can inspire parallel fictional events. For example, an observed low-flying aircraft in a diary may become a thematically related aerial encounter in the probe location. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

Rules:

- Preserve the original meaning of diary entries. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Do not invent diary events. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Do not imply that fictional events literally happened. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Use transformation, parallel and thematic reflection rather than literal copying. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Use diary reminders sparingly and naturally, such as an unfinished Sunday-school leaflet or a birthday notification. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## Continuity record

Each daily chronicle must produce or update a compact continuity record containing:

- Episode position and current working day.
- Principal characters and spotlighted roles.
- Locations and temporal coordinates.
- Edubeam mission status.
- Key discoveries, decisions and incidents.
- Biblical, Health & Safety and news references.
- Open questions and unresolved threads.
- Canon classification for every new element. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

Recommended canon labels:

- **Established canon**
- **Previously established information**
- **Current real-world information**
- **Fictional scenario**
- **Proposed development**
- **Unverified information**
- **Awaiting approval** [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## Editorial safeguards

The AI must not:

- Invent Bible readings, verses, course titles, organisational roles or historical facts. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Misrepresent fictional future news as real reporting. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Contradict the organisational chart or established character histories. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
- Introduce unsupported Edubeam capabilities. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Generate all ten chronicles at once. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Repeat the same morning routine, meeting and mission beats without narrative advancement. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Silently change canon or promote proposals into established facts. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## Manual episode compilation

After ten chronicles exist, a human editor should:

1. Arrange them from Day 1 to Day 10.
2. Check character, role, mission and timeline continuity.
3. Remove repetition and strengthen transitions.
4. Verify all scriptural, safety, news and organisational references.
5. Confirm that the episode has a clear arc.
6. Archive the ten originals, the compiled episode, source references and continuity updates. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## Daily generation prompt

A suitable instruction for any AI is:

> Use the Classies Chronicles Master Episode Framework. First, call the Millennium probe endpoint to identify the current term, the first Monday of that term, and the current working day of the two-week episode. Then review the Classies Chronicles archives, the latest Daily Diary material, the Koware Management repository, the organisational chart and its check days, the Eternal Dreamfall reading plan, the current YouVersion Verse of the Day, relevant Buzzjuice Health & Safety materials, and verified historical/current/probe-period news. Generate **only one Classies Chronicles chronicle** for the identified working day. Make it a single immersive narrative that advances the episode’s storyline, spotlights the applicable role or roles, integrates the spiritual, safety, news, diary and Edubeam threads naturally, and ends with a continuity hook. Do not generate the other nine chronicles. Do not invent dates, readings, roles, news, Edubeam capabilities or canon. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

This update preserves the original’s creative DNA while making the system reproducible, continuity-safe and suitable for any AI to generate a fresh chronicle from the shared links, repositories and archives. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

<<STOP PERPLEXITY-4>>

Below is an updated, generic master framework that replaces the original v0.01.05 daily schedule with a repository-driven production system. It preserves the original’s strongest ideas—ten working days, role rotation, Eternal Dreamfall integration, Health & Safety, news, diary mirroring, and Edubeam missions—while enforcing the key production rules: one chronicle per working day, Monday-to-Friday episode boundaries, and no hardcoded dates, episode numbers, locations, or outcomes. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

# Classies Chronicles — Master Episode Generation Framework

**Proposed version:** CC-MEGF-0.02.00  
**Status:** Proposed update for review and approval  
**Scope:** Generic framework for generating one daily Classies Chronicle at a time  
**Episode length:** Ten working days  
**Episode boundary:** Begins on a Monday; ends on the Friday of the following week  
**Compilation:** The ten daily chronicles are later manually collected, edited, and combined into one episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## 1. Core production rules

1. Generate **only one chronicle per request**, corresponding to the current working day of the active episode.
2. Never generate all ten daily chronicles unless the user explicitly requests a full-episode compilation.
3. Every episode begins on a **Monday** and ends on the following week’s **Friday**.
4. Saturdays and Sundays are non-production days for daily chronicles, although their events may be referenced in the following Monday’s chronicle.
5. Do not hardcode a date, episode number, season, chapter, probe location, mission, headline, character outcome, or resolution.
6. Derive all episode-specific facts from the shared repositories, archives, current-date sources, and the Millennium Timezone endpoint.
7. Preserve established canon; classify new material as proposed, fictional, unverified, or awaiting approval rather than silently making it canon. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 2. Calendar and episode positioning

The generating AI must first determine the current day’s position within the episode before writing.

### 2.1 Term boundaries

Use the Millennium Timezone probe endpoint:

`https://buzzjuice.net/shared/millennium-timezone/millennium-probe.php?probe=now`

It returns `phase_start_iso` and `phase_end_iso`, identifying the current term or chapter phase. From `phase_start_iso`, identify the **first Monday** of the term; that Monday is the start of the term’s first episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### 2.2 Episode-day calculation

After identifying the current Monday-based episode start:

- Days 1–5: Monday through Friday of week one.
- Days 6–10: Monday through Friday of week two.
- If the current date falls on a Saturday or Sunday, do not produce a daily chronicle; instead report that the next production day is the following Monday.
- If the current date precedes the term’s first Monday, report that the episode has not yet commenced.
- If the current date follows the tenth Friday, identify the next episode’s Monday rather than inventing an eleventh day. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### 2.3 Required day header

Every daily chronicle must begin with a neutral production header:

```text
Classies Chronicles — Daily Chronicle
Episode day: [1–10]
Weekday: [Monday–Friday]
Week of episode: [1 or 2]
Term phase: [phase_start_iso to phase_end_iso]
Production status: Single-day chronicle
```

No episode title, date-specific title, or fixed location may be inserted unless it is derived from approved sources. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 3. Source hierarchy

The AI must consult sources in this order of authority:

| Priority | Source | Use |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Classies Chronicles repository and latest Daily Diary entries | Continuity, established canon, character context, outstanding storylines  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 2 | Koware Management repository and organizational chart | Roles, aliases, check days, projects, policies, business context  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 3 | Eternal Dreamfall five-year reading plan | The applicable weekly reading sequence  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 4 | YouVersion Verse of the Day | The actual verse for the current publication date  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 5 | Buzzjuice Health & Safety repository | One relevant, verifiable safety lesson  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 6 | Millennium Timezone endpoints | Historical date, probe date, and current term boundaries  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 7 | Reliable current, local, and historical news sources | Contextual headlines; clearly distinguish real reporting from fictional future reports  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |

If a source is inaccessible, the AI must disclose the limitation and avoid inventing the missing content. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 4. Eternal Dreamfall pipeline

Each daily chronicle must move through one connected creative pipeline, not six disconnected sections:

1. **Eternal Dreamfall Bible Study** — identify the applicable reading from the five-year plan; the plan is organized by week, so the AI must select and clearly identify an appropriate passage from the applicable week rather than invent a daily allocation. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Classies_Chronicles/refs/heads/main/Eternal_Dreamfall_Bible_Study/list_of_biblical_texts-5_year_reading_plan-1.txt)
2. **Eternal Dreamfall Chronicle** — develop the reading’s themes, questions, and moral or spiritual application.
3. **Refreshed Eternal Dreamfall** — connect the theme to one verified Health & Safety lesson from the Buzzjuice repository. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
4. **Headlined Eternal Dreamfall** — integrate relevant historical, current, and probing-period headlines without distorting facts. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
5. **Classies Eternal Dreamfall** — integrate diary notes, archives, character continuity, and Edubeam mission context.
6. **Classies Chronicles** — produce the final immersive daily story, connected to Koware’s live organizational ecosystem. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The final output must read as one narrative, with the biblical, safety, news, diary, and organizational elements influencing decisions, dialogue, and consequences rather than appearing as separate blocks. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 5. Ten-day narrative spine

The ten days should form one evolving episode storyline. The framework defines narrative functions, not mandatory events.

| Day | Narrative function | Typical organizational activity |
|---|---|---|
| 1 — Monday | Establish setting, characters, central situation, assignment, and themes | Weekly briefing; role spotlight begins  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 2 — Tuesday | Begin exploration, research, or Edubeam deployment | Independent work; no compulsory briefing  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 3 — Wednesday | Introduce discovery, complication, or deeper character interaction | Fieldwork, research, mission progress  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 4 — Thursday | Review progress and connect findings to Koware operations | Follow-up briefing  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 5 — Friday | Close week one without resolving the full episode | Reports, security checks, weekend preparation  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 6 — Monday | Re-establish continuity and advance the assignment | Weekly briefing  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 7 — Tuesday | Develop outstanding questions and relationships | Research, mission continuation  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 8 — Wednesday | Converge storylines and expose consequences | Mission or operational turning point  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| 9 — Thursday | Critical decision, retrieval, or major development | Return preparation and reporting  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| 10 — Friday | Conclude, hand over, and preserve continuity | Report integration; next-team preparation  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |

The episode may end with an unresolved thread, but it must provide a meaningful stopping point and record what carries into the next episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 6. Role spotlight and check days

The organizational chart assigns aliases and check days to Koware roles. These check days determine which role is placed in the spotlight on a given episode day. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)

### 6.1 Reading check days

A check day such as `Day 4` means episode day 4. A check day such as `Day 11` means day 1 of the next episode; `Day 15` means day 5 of the next episode; `Day 20` means day 10 of the next episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### 6.2 Spotlight rules

- Focus on one or two spotlight roles per day; use up to four only when the story genuinely requires it.
- Use the role’s alias, responsibilities, department, and check day from the organizational chart.
- Show why the role matters, how it interacts with the Edubeam team, and what the role contributes to Koware.
- A role may recur when the storyline justifies it, but characters must not be inserted solely to meet a quota.
- The Corporate Manager’s routine must remain flexible and diary-driven rather than fixed. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

For example, the chart places the Corporate Secretary, Department Secretary, and Corporate Administrator on Day 2; therefore, a Day 2 chronicle should naturally foreground administrative coordination, records, approvals, or operational support as part of the story. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)

## 7. Daily chronicle structure

Each daily chronicle should be approximately 800–1,500 words, or a comparable short audio/visual script length, and should contain:

1. A cinematic opening grounded in the current day and relevant characters.
2. Morning or workplace routine only where it advances character or plot.
3. The day’s Eternal Dreamfall reading and Verse of the Day integration.
4. One Health & Safety lesson woven into an incident, discussion, commute, workplace task, or mission.
5. Relevant curated headlines or news-inspired developments.
6. Diary-inspired narrative parallels, clearly transformed rather than literally reproduced.
7. Koware role spotlight and organizational development.
8. Edubeam mission progress, where canon supports an active mission.
9. A closing question, riddle, decision point, or continuity hook.
10. A short continuity record for the next generating AI. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

## 8. Edubeam mission rules

The Edubeam is fictional technology and must remain consistent with established project canon. The repository itself identifies EduBeam as fictional and warns that technical explanations are story canon, not real science. [github](https://github.com/cupidblack/Classies_Chronicles)

Each mission must record:

- Mission objective.
- Assigned personnel.
- Probe location and temporal coordinates.
- Deployment status: preparing, deployed, continuing, returning, retrieved, or post-mission.
- Communications status.
- Discoveries, incidents, and unresolved matters.
- Any Koware operational insight gained. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

A mission must not automatically restart each day. The AI must determine from archives whether the team is preparing, deployed, continuing, returning, or undergoing assessment. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 9. News and factual safeguards

- Use reliable sources and verify dates.
- Distinguish event dates from publication dates.
- Label historical headlines by their historical period.
- Label any future or speculative report explicitly as fictional.
- Do not invent Bible verses, YouVersion verses, course titles, organizational roles, quotes, or historical facts.
- Do not present fictional Edubeam science as real-world science. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 10. Continuity record

Every chronicle must end with a compact continuity block:

```text
CONTINUITY UPDATE
- Episode day:
- Central storyline:
- Spotlight roles:
- Biblical reading:
- Verse of the Day:
- Health & Safety lesson:
- Key news threads:
- Edubeam status:
- New canon proposed:
- Outstanding questions:
- Next-day setup:
```

This record allows different AI systems to generate successive days without contradicting one another. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 11. Manual compilation

After all ten chronicles exist, a human editor should:

1. Arrange them from Day 1 to Day 10.
2. Remove repetition and strengthen transitions.
3. Verify character, mission, temporal, biblical, safety, and news continuity.
4. Approve or reject proposed canon.
5. Compile the approved chronicles into one episode.
6. Archive the source references, continuity updates, and editorial decisions. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

## 12. Recommended repository additions

To make the framework portable across AI platforms, add these reusable files to the Classies Chronicles repository:

- `CC_DAILY_CHRONICLE_GENERATION_INSTRUCTIONS.txt`
- `CC_EPISODE_CONTINUITY_RECORD_TEMPLATE.md`
- `CC_SOURCE_REGISTER.md`
- `CC_EPISODE_COMPILATION_TEMPLATE.md`
- `CC_POST_EPISODE_REVIEW_TEMPLATE.md`
- `CC_CANON_CLASSIFICATION_GUIDE.md`

The existing repository already contains foundation, archive, diary, news, reference, and production-support folders, so these files can be integrated without restructuring the project. [github](https://github.com/cupidblack/Classies_Chronicles)

**Governing principle:** the story belongs to the Classies Chronicles universe, not to any individual AI. Each AI generates one day’s chronicle from shared sources, preserves continuity, and leaves a clear record for the next day. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
